cūttiram: 12
tiṇimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinili yilavē;
nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkuta liṉṟeṉa moḻipa
pulaṉaṉ kuṇarnta pulami yōrē.
karuttu: ituvum itiyaṭutta uripporuḷallaṉa eṉṉuñ cūttiramum muṉ aintāvatu cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya mulli mutaliya nilavakikaḷukkum, piṉ puṇartal pirital eṉum 14āvatu cūttirattiṟ kūṟum kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇivaki kaḷukkumuḷḷa iyipu muraṇkaḷi, naṭuniliviḷakkāy niṉṟu, eṭuttukkāṭṭi aiyamakaṟṟa eḻunta cūttiraṅkaḷākum.poruḷ: tiṇi mayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭinili yilavē = kuṟiñci mutaliya aṉpiṉin tiṇikaḷāṉa puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉṟa aintoḻukkaṅkaḷum tattamakkuc ciṟappurimi yuṭiya muṟiyē kuṟiñci pāli mulli neytal marutam eṉṉum nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻvatuṭaṉ, avvāṟu ciṟappurimi yaṟṟa piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vantu kalattalum vilakkappaṭā; nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkutal iṉṟeṉa moḻipa = (ivvāṟoḻukkaṅkaḷ tamak kurimiyaṟṟa nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻntu mayaṅkutalamiyu meṉiṉum) avvoḻukka mayakkam paṟṟit tammuḷ nilaṅkaḷ mayaṅkutalilli yeṉṟu kūṟuvar; pulaṉ naṉkuṇarnta pulamiyōrē = pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkaṅkaḷi naṉkaṟinta aṟivuṭiyōr.
kuṟippu: itaṉ mutal mūṉṟāmaṭikaḷiṉ īṟṟēkāraṅkaḷ acinili. ummi tiṇikkum mayakkam ciṟappiṉmi uṇarttum. itaṉāl ovvoru nilattiṟkum ovvorutiṇi ciṟappurimi yuṭiyateṉpatum, avvurimi muṟiyaṉṟi nilaṅkaḷun tiṇi kaḷum tammuḷ mayaṅkutal ciṟavāteṉiṉum vilakkumāṟilli eṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. mayakkam, taṉakkuc ciṟappurimiyaṟṟa nilattil piṟa oḻukka nikaḻcci kuṟikkum; ennilattum tiṇikaḷ tammuṭṭā mayaṅkutal kūṭāmiyiṉ, atu karuttaṉmi tēṟṟam. oruṅku, uṭaṉikaḻccip poruṭṭu.
akattuṟikaḷil mutalil oḻukkaṅkaḷukkē uriya kuṟiñci mutaliya peyarkaḷ piṉ niralē avvoḻukkaṅkaḷukkuc ciṟanturiya nilaṅkaḷukkum ākupeyarāy vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. avikaḷ nilaṅ kaḷukkē uriya peyarkaḷeṉal piṟkālap piṟaḻvuṇarcci. eṉavē, ciṟappuk kuṟiyākap puṇartal mutaliya aintiṇikaḷiyum, āku peyarāy avaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟanturiya nilaṅkaḷiyum, kuṟiñci pāli mulli neytal marutam eṉum potuppeyarkaḷāṟ kūṟutal paḻiya tamiḻ nūṉ marapu. eṉil, peyaroṟṟumiyāl tiṇi kaḷum nilaṅkaḷum yāṇṭum oru nīrmiya veṉak koṇṭu, tiṇimayaṅkuntōṟum nilamayakkamum uṇṭeṉak koḷḷutal kūṭāteṉa vaṟpuṟutti aiyamakaṟṟuvatē īṇṭiccūttirak karuttākum. tiṇiyeṉpatu oḻukkattiṟkē ciṟanturiya peyarākum. tiṇik kuripporuḷē eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. ovvōroḻukkamum avvavataṟkuc ciṟappurimi kūṟiya oru nilattiṟkē taṉiyurimi yuṭittaṉṟu. ovvoru nilamakkaḷum avvan nilattil aintiṇiyoḻukkaṅkaḷiyum kiyāḷutaliyalpu. ākavē, ovvoru nilattum atuvataṟkuc ciṟanturiya oḻukkamē yaṉṟip piṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷiyum toṭarpupaṭuttip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañceytal tavaṟākātu. oru nilattu nikaḻum oḻukka vēṟu pāṭṭāl annilattiyalpum māṟiṉatākak karutalākātu. mayaṅki nikaḻum oḻukkattōṭu oruṅkē nilamayakkaṅ koḷḷutal marapaṉṟu eṉpatum, ovvoru nilattiṟkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiyatākak kūṟappaṭiṉum atukoṇṭu  annilattu avvōr oḻukkamē nikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟiyiṉṟi nilattiyal karutāmal tiṇimayakkam (atāvatu piṟavoḻukka nikaḻcci) kūṟutalum marapeṉpatum, tolkāppiyar iccūttirattāl viḷakkip pōntār. eṉavē, eṅku evvoḻukkam nikaḻiṉum ataṉāl nilattiyal māṟātu; kāṭu puṇarcci nikaḻvatāl kuṟiñciyākātu, mulli nilamēyām. pirivāṟṟāk kuṟamakaḷiriraṅkuvatāl mali nilam neytalākātu; kuṟiñciyēyām.
tattam iyalmāṟāta nilam yātāyiṉum, atil nikaḻum oḻukkampaṟṟit tiṇi vakuppatu paḻiya marapu; oḻukka iyal karutātu, nilavakiyāl tiṇiyamippatu piḻipaṭṭa piṟkāla vaḻakku. atu tolkāppiyarkkuṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu eṉpatu ic cūttirattāl teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. puṇartal - mutaliya ovvoru tiṇi allatu oḻukkattiṟkum, muṟiyē mali mutaliya nilaṅ kaḷip pōlavē, kūtiryāma mutaliya kāla vakikaḷum tattam iyaṟporuttam paṟṟit taṉicciṟappurimi koḷḷumeṉiṉum, oru tiṇikku orē kālantāṉ kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉum variyaṟi
yilli. entat tiṇiyum taṉ iyalpaṟṟi ataṟkuc ciṟanturiya tallāta piṟanilam piṟa kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻtal kūṭumātaliṉ, ovvoru tiṇiyum ellānilaṅkaḷilum pōlavē ellāk kālaṅkaḷoṭum kalantu nikaḻvatum kaṭiyappaṭātu eṉpatum veḷippaṭi. eṉavē, ovvōroḻukkamum ataṟkuc ciṟanturiya tākak kūṟappeṟṟa nilam poḻutukaḷilēyē nikaḻum eṉa oru taliyāk koḷḷal kūṭātu; uripporuḷkaḷākiya ellā oḻukkaṅkaḷum, nilamum poḻutumākiya mutaṟporuḷ vakikaḷil orōvoṉṟit tattamakku iyaliyipupaṟṟic ciṟappurimi koḷḷiṉum, ellā nilaṅkaḷiṉum ellāp poḻutukaḷiṉum ettiṇiyum ēṟṟa peṟṟi kalantu nikaḻtaluṅ kaṭiyap paṭātu eṉpatē iccūttirak karuttātal teḷivākum. iṉi, iccūttirattil mutaṟporuḷiraṇṭil poḻutukūṟāmal nilam vitantu kūṟap paṭṭatu. poḻutu pōlaṉṟit tiṇiyoṭu nilam kuṟiñci mutaliya potuppeyar peṟṟu mayaṅkutalāl varum aiyamakaṟṟaṟ poruṭṭu, tiṇiyoṭu peyaroṟṟumiyillāta poḻutukaḷ paṟṟi attakiya mayakkattiṟ kiṭaṉilliyātaliṉ, poḻutu mayaṅkāmi vitantu kūṟavēṇṭiṟṟilli.
itaṟku oru nilattu iraṇṭuripporuḷ, atāvatu iraṇ ṭoḻukkam tammuṉ mayaṅkutalaṉṟi, iraṇṭu nilam orō voḻukkattiṉkaṇ mayaṅkutalilli eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. mēlum, uripporuḷ mayakkuṟutal eṉṉātu, tiṇi mayakkuṟutalumeṉṟār, ōruripporuḷoṭu ōrurip poruḷ mayaṅkutalum.... ivvāṟē kālamayaṅkutalum, karup poruḷ mayaṅkutalum peṟumeṉṟaṟku eṉac ciṟappukkuṟippum kūṭṭivirittār. itaṉāl oru nilattorukālattu oṉṟaṉmikka palatiṇikaḷ mayaṅkumeṉpatu karuttāyiṉ, atu kūṭāmi tēṟṟam. aṉṟi orutiṇi pala nilattum mayaṅkum iyalpiyē ivvāṟu kūṟiṉar eṉiṉ, atu mikiyāvatōṭu vēṟupāṭillāta oru māṟupāṭṭit tāṉēpaṭittu mayaṅkuvatumākum. ovvoru nilattum ataṟkuriya vallāp piṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷ nikaḻtalamiyum  eṉpataṉālēyē, ovvōroḻukkattiṟkum ataṟkuriya nilamē yaṉṟip piṟanilaṅkaḷiyun toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟutal amivuṭit teṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. oru nilattup pala voḻukkam nikaḻum eṉṟapiṉ, palanilattu ōroḻukkam nikaḻum (atāvatu ovvō roḻukkattōṭum pala nilattoṭarpu  amiyum) eṉpati vilakkumāṟilli.eṉavē, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatē īṇṭuk karuttāyiṉ, tiṇimayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭiniliyilavē eṉṟa aḷavē akkarutti viḷakkap pōtiyatākum; nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkutaliṉṟu eṉak kūṭṭiyurippatu poruṭ poruttamiṉṟi muraṇpāṭum viḷittu mayaṅka vikkum. iṉi, oruppaṭṭa talimakkaḷuḷ ōroḻukkam pala nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻvatu oru kālat tamiyātu eṉṟu kūṟi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urikku amivu kāṭṭuvatum poruntātu. orēkālattum orunilattum pala voḻukkam oruṅku nikaḻtalum, oruppaṭṭa orē talimakkaḷuḷ ōroḻukkam orukālattup palaviṭattu nikaḻtalum kūṭāmi veḷippaṭi. ataṉālum atu poruḷaṉmiyaṟika.
itaṟku iḷampūraṇar kūṟum poruḷāvatu: oru tiṇik kuriya mutaṟporuḷ maṟṟōr tiṇikkuriya mutaṟporuḷoṭu cēraniṟṟaluṅ kaṭiyappaṭātu; āṇṭu nilam cēraniṟṟalilli yeṉṟu colluvar; eṉavē kālam mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉpatē. cūttirattil oru nilattiṟkuriya voḻukkam vēṟoru nilattiṟkuk kūṟutalum kaṭiniliyilaveṉat teḷḷatteḷiya viḷakki yirukkavum, ataṟku māṟāka orutiṇikkuriya mutaṟ poruḷ maṟṟōr tiṇikkuriya mutaṟporuḷōṭu cēra niṟṟal kūṭātu eṉa muraṇap poruḷ kūṟuvatu amivuṭittaṉṟu. tolkāppiyar tiṇimayakkuṟutal eṉṟāraṉṟi, tiṇiyil mutaṟporuḷ mayaṅkutal eṉṟu kūṟiṉārilli. ātaliṉ, iḷampūraṇar kūṟṟum iccūttirac coṟṟoṭarukku nēriya poruḷaṉṟu.
tiṇikaḷiyum nilaṅkaḷiyum cuṭṭi avaṟṟiṉiyipu viḷakka eḻunta iccūttirattil, avaṟṟiṉ puṟampāṉa kālaṅkaḷi yum karupporuḷkaḷiyum pukutta vēṇṭip paḻiya, urikārar iccūttirattiṟkut tammuḷ māṟupaṭap palavāṟu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamāṉa poruntāp poruḷ kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
tiṇi mayakkuṟutaṟkuc ceyyuḷ.
	kaṭaṟkāṉaṟ cērppa! kaḻiyulāay nīṇṭa
	aṭaṟkāṉaṟ puṉṉitāḻn tāṟṟa - maṭaṟkāṉal
	aṉṟi lakavum aṇineṭum peṇṇittem
	muṉṟi liḷamaṇaṉmēl moyttu	(tiṇimāli 190. cey. 56)
itil kuṟiñcit tiṇiyiṉ iravukkuṟi nērumtuṟi, taṉakkuriya kuṟiñci nilattaṉṟi, iraṅkaṟṟiṇikkuriya ney taṉilattu mayaṅkiyataṟika. avvāṟu kuṟiñcittiṇi vantu kalappiṉum, ataṉāl nilattiyal mayaṅkāmal neytalākavē niṟpatu kūṟap paṭutalum kāṇka. iṉṉum, pulālakaṟṟum eṉum tiṇimāli (35ām) veṇpāvum neytaṉilattil kuṟiñcittiṇi mayakkam kūṟum. iṉi,
	puṉpuṟavē cēvalō ṭūṭal poruḷaṉṟāl,
	aṉpuṟa vēyuṭiyā rāyiṉum - vaṉpuṟ
	ṟatukāṇ akaṉṟa vaḻinōkkip poṉpōrt
	titukāṇeṉ vaṇṇa miṉi	(tiṇimāli 152l. cey. 74)
eṉum tiṇimālippāṭṭil neytaṟṟiṇi pālinilattu mayaṅkutal aṟika.
cūttiram: 13
uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē.
karuttu: itu aintiṇikaḷ pōlavē, attiṇikaḷiṉ cārpāṉa piṟa uripporuḷkaḷum pala nilaṅkaḷilum ēṟṟapeṟṟi vantu mayaṅkum, eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: uripporuḷallaṉa = tiṇikkurip poruḷē eṉa variyaṟutta puṇartal mutal aintiṇiyallāta uripporu ḷāṉa talimakkaḷ oḻukkamāvaṉa piṟa; mayaṅkavumpeṟum = ennilattum vantu kalattalumuṇṭu.
kuṟippu: puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉṉum tiṇikkuripporuḷ aintumaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ cārpāy avvurip poruḷkaḷōṭu amittuk kōṭaṟpālaṉavāy, piṟṟic cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟum koṇṭutalik kaḻital pirintava ṇiraṅkal, kalanta poḻutu. kāṭci mutaliyaṉavum, aintiṇi uripporuḷkaḷip pōlavē oru nilattukkut taṉiyurimi yiṉṟi ellānilattum vantu mayaṅkavum peṟum. īṟṟēkāram acinili.
tiṇikkurip poruḷkaḷāka vitantu kūṟum puṇartal mutaliya aintum enta nilattum vantu mayaṅkutal mēṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭatu. pēraḷavil avvintaṉuḷaṭaṅkāmal, iyalpaṟṟi uripporuḷā yamivaṉa piṟavumuḷa eṉpati ivviyal koṇṭu talikkaḻital kalantapoḻutum eṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cuṭṭuvatāl, attakiya piṟa akavurip poruḷkaḷum, vitantōtiya aintiṇi yuripporuḷkaḷ pōlavē, variyaṟiyiṉṟi yenta nilattum vantu mayaṅkutal kūṭum eṉpati intac cūttiram viḷakkukiṟatu. īṇṭu uripporuḷallaṉa eṉpatu muṉṉic cūttirattil tiṇikkuripporuḷ eṉa vitantōtiya talimakkaḷoḻukkam aintu mallāta piṟaveṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. uripporu ḷallaṉa eṉpati allaṉa uripporuḷ eṉac coṉmāṟṟi, muṉvitantōtiya aintumallāta uripporuḷāvaṉa eṉak koḷḷuvatē īṇṭup peritum poruttamuṭittām.
iṉi, ivvāṟu koḷḷāmal, uripporuḷallāta karup poruḷum mutaṟporuḷum piṟatiṇiyōṭu cērum eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar poruḷ koḷvatum poruntātu. ēṉeṉil, tiṇimayakkuṟutalum eṉṟa cūttirattāl mutaṟ poruḷ mayakka mum ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvumpuḷḷum eṉpatāl karupporuḷ kaḷiṉ mayakkamum muṉ taṉi vēṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, avaṟṟiyē iccūttirattilum tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉāreṉpatu kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku avariyāḷākkum.
uripporuḷ eṉṟōtiya aintiṇiyu mallāta kik kiḷi peruntiṇiyum nālvaki nilattum mayaṅkavum peṟum eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyum amivuṭittaṉṟu. kik kiḷi peruntiṇikaḷukku nilam poḻutukaḷil etuvumē taṉiyurimi kūṟātatāl, vilakkillā nilampoḻutukaḷil avi vantu mayaṅkumeṉac cuṭṭutal mikiyākum. aṉṟiyum mēl iraṇṭāñ cūttira mutal kīḻ 42ām cūttiramuṭiya niralē naṭuvaṇin tiṇikaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷiyē viḷakkip, piṟaku 43 mutal 49 variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil cila potuviyalpukaḷ kūṟi, avaṟṟiṉ piṉ ivviyaliṟutiyil 50, 51ām cūttiraṅkaḷil kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷi viḷakki muṭikkum innūlār īṇṭu aṉpiṉin tiṇiyiṉ iyalpukaḷukkiṭiyē iṉṟiyamiyāt toṭarpu etuvumiṉṟi iṟutiyiṟ kūṟum kikkiḷi peruntiṇi kaḷiyiḻuttu avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟumuṉ mayakkaṅ kūṟiṉā reṉak koḷḷutal evvāṟṟāṉuñ cālāmiyaṟika.
aintiṇiyallāp piṟa uripporuḷ palanilattu mayaṅkutaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
koṇṭutalikkaḻital attakiya piṟa uripporuḷāta luṇṭeṉa aṭutta piṉ cūttiram kūṟukiṟatu. avvuripporuḷ puṇartaṟkuriya kuṟiñci nilattil mayaṅkuvatākak kīḻvarum paḻam pāṭṭuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
niṉiyāy, vāḻi, tōḻi! eṉum pūtantēvaṉār kuṟumpāṭṭil,
		.............. pulippakuvā yēṟṟi
		.............viṭarmukik
		kōṭi oṟṟiyakaruṅkālvēṅka
		vāṭupūñciṉiyiṟkiṭakkum
		uyarvaranāṭaṉoṭupeyarumāṟē”(kuṟun. 343)
puli vēṅkic ciṉiyiṟ kiṭakku muyarvari nāṭākavē nilam kuṟiñci; nāṭaṉoṭu peyartal koṇṭutalik kaḻitalā muripporuḷ. iṉṉum ūuralareḻa eṉum peruṅkaṭuṅ kōviṉ pāṭṭu matuvātalaṟika.
	uṇalāyn ticiṉā lavaroṭu cēynāṭṭu
	viṇṭoṭa nivanta vilaṅkumalik kavāṟ
	karumpunaṭu pātti yaṉṉa
	peruṅkaḻiṟ ṟaṭivaḻi niliiya nīrē           	(kuṟun.262)
itil, puṇartaṟkuriya maliyiṟ ṟalivaṉuṭaṉ pōkum talivi kaḷiṟṟaṭi vaḻi niliiya nīrit talivaṉō ṭuṇṇutali virumpum oḻukkam mayaṅkutalaṟika.
iṉi, koṇṭutalikkaḻital,
	aṉṉāy vāḻi vēṇṭaṉṉi, nampaṭappit
	tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya, avarnāṭ
	ṭuvalik kūvaṟ kīḻa
	māṉuṇ ṭeñciya kaluḻi nīrē	(aiṅ. 203)
eṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟup pāṭṭil kuṟiñci nilattum,
	oṉṟāṉu nāmmoḻiya lāmō celavutāṉ
	piṉṟātu pēṇum pukaḻāṉpiṉ - piṉṟā
	velaṟkaritām vilvalāṉ vēlviṭali pāṅkāc
	celaṟkaritāc cēya curam	(tiṇimāli 150l cey. 87)
eṉum tiṇimāli veṇpāvil pālinilattum, vantu mayaṅkutal kāṇka.
itupōlavē maṟṟiya aintiṇiyilaṭaṅkā uripporuḷ aṉittum  ellānilattum mayaṅkumeṉṟaṟika.
cūttiram: 14
puṇartal pirita lirutta liraṅkal
ūṭa livaṟṟi ṉimitta meṉṟivi
tēruṅ kālit tiṇikkurip poruḷē.
karuttu: iccūttiram akapporuṭ pakutiyiṟ ciṟanta aṉpiṉintiṇikku nērurimikoṇṭa oḻukka vakikaḷi uṇarttukiṟatu.
poruḷ: puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭalivaṟṟiṉ nimitta meṉṟivi = kūṭutal, pirital, piriviṭiyāṟṟi yiruttal, āṟṟātiraṅkal, pulavi eṉṟa aintum avaṟṟiṟ kiyipuṭiya nimittaṅkaḷumē; tēruṅkālit tiṇikkuripporuḷē = ārāyum poḻutu aṉpiṉintiṇiyeṉaṟkuc ciṟanturiya poruḷkaḷām.
kuṟippu: itil īṟṟēkāram tēṟṟamākum. mutal karu urip poruḷkaḷaṉittum akattiṇip pakutiyākavē amittuk kōṭal tavaṟaṉṟāyiṉum, tiṇiyeṉpatu oḻukkaṅkaṇṇiya pērātalāl aṉpiṉintiṇi yeṉaṟkup puṇartal pirital mutaliya talimakkaḷ aintoḻukkaṅkaḷē ciṟappurimiyuṭiya poruḷkaḷākum eṉpati iccūttiram viḷakkukiṟatu.
nimittamāvatu ovvōroḻukkatti yaṭuttu muṉṉum piṉṉum mutalum muṭivumākat toṭuttu avvoḻukkattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāt toṭarpuṭiyaṉavaṟṟic cuṭṭuvatām. pukumukam purital, nakunayam maṟittal mutaliyaṉa puṇartalukku muṉṉeḻum nimittaṅkaḷām. aṇintavi tiruttal, pārāṭṭeṭuttal mutaliyaṉa puṇarviṉ piṉṉar nikaḻum nimittaṅkaḷām. ivvāṟu toṭar paṇimi yaṟṟaṉa nimittamākā.
aṉṉapiṟavum... nimittameṉpa eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyal 19ām cūttiramum iti valiyuṟuttum. ivvāṟē piṟatiṇikaḷukkum nimitta  vakikaḷi ēṟṟa peṟṟi amittuk koḷka. niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē muṉṉar mutaṟporuḷik kūṟiṉavar ataṉi yaṭuttuk karupporuḷik kūṟāmal ataṟkumuṉ īṇṭurip poruḷkaḷik kūṟuvatēṉeṉiṟ colluvaṉ: akap pakutiyil mutaṟporuḷ uripporuḷkaḷ allātaṉa aṉittum karup poruḷkaḷā yamitaliṉ, mutalil variyaṟippaṭṭavaṟṟi viḷakkuvāṉ ṟoṭaṅki, nilampoḻuteṉum iraṇṭē vakiyu ḷaṭaṅkum mutaṟporuḷ kūṟiṉatum, aḷavaṟutippaṭāk karup poruḷkaḷik kūṟumuṉ, aintu oḻukkattaḷavil aṭaṅkum urip poruḷi iṭiyiṟkūṟiyamittapeṟṟi uyttuṇara vittār. akavakic ceyyuḷkaḷaṉittum ivvintiṇiyē kūṟutaliṉ, eṭuttukkāṭṭu īṇṭikkuvēṇṭā.
cūttiram: 15
koṇṭutalik kaḻitalum pirintava ṇiraṅkalum
uṇṭeṉa moḻipa ōriṭat tāṉa.
karuttu: itu, mēliccūttirattil tiṇikkurip poru ḷeṉa viḷakkiya aintaṉuḷaṭaṅkātaṉavāyt, tammiyalpāl akat tiṇikaḷuk kuripporuḷkaḷāy āṭci peṟum piṟa cilavum uṇṭeṉak kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: koṇṭutalik kaḻitalum = kaḷavil talivaṉ taliviyit taṉṉuṭaṉ koṇṭucēṟalum; pirintavaṇiraṅkalum  = avvāṟuṭaṉ koṇṭu celluṅkāl taliviyiṉ tamarvaravu mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl piriyanēriṉ āṇṭap pirivupaṟṟi yiraṅkalum; uṇṭeṉa moḻipa = ēṟṟapeṟṟi ivi urip poruḷpōla varutalum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar (pulavar); ōriṭat tāṉa = avi akattiṇik kiyipuṭiya orōviṭattu.
kuṟippu: koṇṭutalik kaḻital eṉpataṉāl, talimakkaḷ tammuḷ atu piritalākātu; nērē puṇartalum aṉṟām. atu nilam peyartaliṉ puṇartaliṉ aṭaṅkāmiyāṉum, uṭaṉ koṇṭu peyartaliṉ piritaliṉaṭaṅkāmiyāṉum, vēṟu ōtap paṭṭatu eṉum iḷampūraṇar kuṟippu īṇṭuc cintikkattakkatu. iṉi, atuvē pōl pirintavaṇiraṅkal eṉpatum pirintavar iraṅkal eṉṉuntiṇikkurip poruḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭatu eṉpati viḷakkutaṟkākavē, koṇṭutalik kaḻiyumiṭattup piriya nērntuḻi avaṇ iraṅkal eṉa vitantu viḷakkappeṟṟatu. iccūttiram cuṭṭum iraṇṭum akattuṟikaḷil uripporuḷkaḷākiya tali makkaḷiṉoḻukkamākavē āḷappeṟutalāṉum, ivi puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉṟa tiṇikkuripporuḷ kaḷintaṉuḷ etaṉilum aṭaṅkāmiyāṉum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum vēṟu pirittu, iccūttirattāl orōviṭattu uripporuḷātaṟku uriyaṉa iviyeṉṟu teḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ituvē tolkāppiyar karutteṉpatu piṉ ivviyalil oṉṟāttamariṉum eṉum (41ām) cūttirattil, iṭic curamaruṅki lavaḷtamareytik kaṭikkoṇṭu peyartaliṟ kalaṅkaña reyti eṉṟavar viḷakkutalāṉumaṟika.
īṇṭu iḷampūraṇar koṇṭutalik kaḻitalip pālik kaṇṇum pirintavaṇiraṅkalip peruntiṇik kaṇṇum varum uripporuḷkaḷākkip poruḷ kūṟuvar. uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal (atāvatu koṇṭu talikkaḻital) piriviṉaṭaṅkātu eṉa ivarē kūṟiyapiṉ, atu pirivām pālik kaṇvarumeṉal, taṉṉoṭutāṉē māṟukoḷak kūṟuvatām. talimakkaḷ tammuṭ pirintiraṅkuvatu nēriya neytaṟṟiṇik kuripporuḷākum. pirintiraṅkal eṉittāṉum peruntiṇippāṟ paṭutalilli, atu ottakātaloḻukkamātaliṉ.
iṉi, ivviraṇṭiyum tanniliyil uripporuḷāk kāmal, tiṇimayakkam eṉṟu koṇṭu pālittiṇiyuḷ kuṟiñciyum neytalumākiya piṟatiṇikaḷiṉ mayakkam kūṟuvatākak koḷḷum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyum poruntātu. tiṇi mayakkuṟutal eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarāl mayaṅki, orutiṇiyuḷ piṟatiṇikaḷ oruṅku vantu mayaṅkumeṉak koḷḷutal evvāṟ ṟāṉum poruntātu. talimakkaḷiruvaruḷ orukālattōroḻukkam nikaḻvataṉṟip palavoḻukkam orukālattukkalantu mayaṅku meṉpatu iyalpaṉṟākaliṉ, ataṟkilakkaṇamum vēṇṭappaṭā. oru nilattiṟ palavoḻukkaṅkaḷum ōroḻukkam palanilaṅkaḷilum vēṟupaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻumeṉpatiyē muṉ tiṇimayakkuṟutal eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl viḷakkiṉāraṉṟi, orukālattup pala
voḻukkan tammuḷ mayaṅkumeṉṟu tolkāppiyar yāṇṭum kūṟilar, aḵtiyalpaṉmiyiṉ. ataṉāl iccūttiram tiṇimayakkam kūṟuvataṉṟu, orucār uripporuḷāmpiṟa cila kūṟutalē nutaliṟṟu eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. aṉṟiyum itil tiṇimayakkam kūṟutalē avar karuttāyiṉ itaṉiyum itupōṉṟa tiṇimayakkam nutalum piṟacūttiraṅkaḷiyum tiṇikkuripporuḷ kūṟuñ cūttirattiṟkumuṉ tiṇimayakkuṟutalum uripporuḷallaṉa eṉpavaṟṟōṭu iyipu nōkki iṇittuk kūṟiyiruppar. avvāṟaṉṟi, tiṇikkuripporuḷ vaki aintiyuṅ kūṟuñ cūttirattiṟkup piṉ itu kūṟappaṭutalāl, ituvum avvintaṉuḷaṭaṅkāta piṟa uripporuḷ vaki viḷakku vatiyē nutaliṟṟeṉpatu tēṟṟam.
īṇṭu ummi yiraṇṭum eṇṇummi.
koṇṭutalik kaḻitalukkuc ceyyuḷ, muṉ uripporu ḷallaṉa eṉum (13ām) cūttira urikkuṟippil kāṭṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷik kāṇka.
pirintavaṇiraṅkalukkuc ceyyuḷ:
	numarvari ṉōrppi ṉallatu, amarvariṉ
	munnīr maṇṭila  maṉittu māṟṟāt
	terikaṇi viṭuttalō vilaṉē:
	arimatar maḻikkaṇ kaluḻvaki evaṉō?
ippaḻiya uri mēṟkōṭ ceyyuḷil, tamar varavu karuti ataṉāṉērum piriviṉukkañci uṭaṉpōkkil talimakaḷiraṅkat talivaṉ tēṟṟuṅ kuṟippaṟika.
	ētaṅ karutā tiḷiyōynī eṉṉōṭu
	pōtaru vāykol pukaleṉṟa - kātalarpiṉ
	vantēṉi viṭṭu maṟintār; emarveṟuttēṟ
	kentanili eytu miṉi!
eṉum veṇpāvil, uṭaṉpōkkiṭiyil toṭarun talivi tamari avaḷmuṉ iṭaruṟap poruvati veṟuttut talivaṉ maṟiya, taṟkoḷavanta tamarimaṟuttuk kaṟpu vaṟpuṟuttun talivi yiṉirakkam kūṟappeṟutal kāṇka.
cūttiram: 16
kalanta poḻutuṅ kāṭciyu maṉṉa.
karuttu: ituvum, aintiṇi vakuppil aṭaṅkātaṉavāy akappakutiyil uripporuḷ āvaṉa iṉṉuñ cilavaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
poruḷ: kalanta poḻutum = otta talimakkaḷ tammiṭip pālatāṇiyiṉ mutal etirppāṭṭil nikaḻvum; (kalanta) kāṭciyum = avvāṟu talippaṭṭār tammuḷ aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kūṭṭik kuṟippurikkum nāṭṭamum; aṉṉa = (mēliccūttirattiṟ kūṟiyatu pōla) ēṟpuḻi akavoḻukkattiṟku uripporuḷātaṟkuriyavākum.
kuṟippu: ummi yiraṇṭum muṉṉic cūttirattil ōtiyavaṟ ṟuṭaṉ vitteṇṇattakum  eṉpatik kuṟippatāl iṟantatu taḻīiya eccavummi. aṉṉa eṉpatu, mēṟkūṟiya koṇṭu talik
kaḻital pirintavaṇiraṅkal eṉpaṉavaṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟiya kalanta
poḻutum kāṭciyum uripporuḷiyalpāl oppaṉa eṉpatic cuṭṭum kuṟippu muṟṟām.  kalanta eṉṉum eccattik kāṭci yeṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. īṇṭup poḻutu kālam kāṭṭātu, talippaṭṭuḻi nikaḻccikaḷukku ākupeyarām. mutaṟporuḷāṉa kālam muṉṉarē kūṟi muṭintatāl atu mīṇṭum kūṟal vēṇṭā.
iṅkuk, kalantapoḻu teṉpatu piṉkaḷaviyal iraṇṭāñ cūttiram kūṟum talimakkaḷiṉ taliyetirppāṭṭiyum, kāṭci eṉpatu akkaḷaviyal aintāñ cūttiram kūṟum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kuṟippunāṭum nōkkattiyum muṟiyē kuṟikkum. kalattal eṉpatu talippaṭutal eṉṟa poruḷatākum. orumūvēṅ kalanta kāli eṉṉuṅ kamparaṭiyum, kalantu pōrceytā rōrcilar eṉṟa kantapurāṇa aṭiyuṅ kāṇka. kalappum eṉṉātu kalanta poḻutum eṉṟatu mutaletirppaṭuṅkālattu nikaḻum kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu mutaliya palavum aṭaṅkutaṟporuṭṭu. iccūttirattil kāṭciyeṉpatu mutaliṟ kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyun talippaṭṭuk kāṇpataṉṟu; atu kalantapoḻutu eṉpatil aṭaṅkum. etippaṭṭa talimakkaḷiṭi avar aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kūṭṭiyurikkum kuṟippuri nāṭum nōkkattiyē īṇṭuk kāṭciyeṉṟār. piṉ kaḷaviyalil viḷakkappaṭumivi yiraṇṭum puṇartal mutaliya aintiṇikaḷuḷ eviyumākā veṉiṉum, akattuṟippāṭṭukkaḷuḷ iviyum uripporuḷ kaḷāka āṭci peṟuvatāl, aintiṇikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkāta uripporuḷ uṇmikūṟu mivviṭattilivi iyipu paṟṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
	kūṟṟamō kaṇṇō piṇiyō maṭavaral
	nōkkamim mūṉṟu muṭittu
eṉuṅkuṟaḷil talimakkaḷiṉ mutaṟṟalippāṭṭil iruvarum tammuḷ aṟivuṭampaṭuttaṟkuk kuṟippaṟiya nāṭṭamiraṇṭum kūṭṭiyurikkum peṟṟi viḷakkappaṭutal kāṇka. muṉṉalum muyaṟciyumiṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyt talippaṭal kalanta poḻutākum; talippaṭṭavar, oruvar maṟṟavar kuṟippaṟiyanāṭum nōkku kalanta kāṭci (aṟiviṉāṭṭam) ām kuṟikkoṇṭu nōkkuntalivaṉ, talimakaḷ kuṟippunōkkā lavaḷ karuttum taṉṉatēpōṟ kātal kaṇṇiyateṉakkaṇṭu taṉakkuttāṉ tēṟṟumavaṉkūṟṟil, aiyamum, accamum teḷintu makiḻtalum oruṅku kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
mutalil avaḷkarut taṟiyāmiyā lavaṉ añcuvatum, accamakaṟṟu mavaḷ aḷinōkkum, ataṉmēliruvarumaṟivuṭampaṭṭuḷḷam puṇarntuḻi avaṉavaḷ nalam pārāṭṭalum ākiya mūṉṟu miṉitu viḷaṅkum. kūṟṟamō eṉṟatālaccamum, kaṇṇō eṉṟatāl kaṇṇōṭumavaḷ kātal nōkkum, piṇiyō eṉṟatāl aṟivuṭampaṭ ṭuḷaṅkalantuḻi avaṉavaḷip pārāṭṭalum iṉitamiyac ciṟukuṟaḷiṟ ceṟitta cevvi viyattaṟkurittu.
	aṇaṅkukol, āymayil kollō kaṉaṅkuḻi
	mātarkol mālumeṉ ṉeñcu
eṉpatilum, aṇaṅkā liyavaccamum, mātar eṉpatālaṟivuṭam pāṭṭut teḷivum, mayil eṉpatāl pārāṭṭu moruṅkami talaṟika.
	mayilkol, maṭavāḷkol, mānīrt tiriyuṭ
	payilvatōr teyvaṅkol kēḷīr kuyilpayiruṅ
	kaṉṉi yiḷañāḻaṟ pūmpoḻil nōkkiya
	kaṇṇiṉ varuttumeṉ ṉeñcu	(tiṇimoḻi. 49)
ittiṇimoḻi veṇpāvum, neytaṉilattuk kalanta poḻutum kāṭciyumāya uripporuḷ mayakkam kūṟukiṟatu.
cūttiram: 17
mutaleṉap paṭuva tāyiru vakittē.
karuttu: iḵtu, uripporuḷ karupporuḷkaḷ pōla vilakkukaḷ vēṇṭi viriyāmal, mutaṟporuḷ nilampoḻu tiraṇṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉpati valiyuṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
poruḷ: mutaleṉappaṭuvatu = mutaṟporuḷ eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuvatu. āyiruvakittē = mēlē nālāvatu cūttirattil kūṟiyāṅku nilampoḻutiraṇṭi ṉiyalpeṉum iraṇṭē vakikaḷi yāṇṭum uṭiyatākum.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram tēṟṟaṅkuṟikkum. mutal, karu, uri eṉṟa akapporuṭ pakutikaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ uripporuḷāvaṉa aintoḻukkamum, avipōla akattuṟiyiṟ ciṟanturiya tali makkaḷiṉ kātaloḻukkaṅkaḷ piṟa cilavumāka vakipeṟu
meṉpati innūlār mēṟ cūttiraṅkaḷil viḷakkiṉār. atu
pōlavē karupporuḷkaḷum palavakippaṭumeṉpati iṉi varuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟuvar. iviyiraṇṭum pōlātu, yāṇṭum mutaṟporuḷāvaṉa nilamum poḻutumeṉṟiraṇṭē  vakikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉpati iyipu nōkki ivviṭic cūttirattāl aiyamaṟa variyaṟuttu vaṟpuṟuttukiṟār. iḵtu iṟantatu kāttal, kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal, muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉṉum uttikaḷāl amiyu mātaliṉ, kūṟiyatu kūṟalākātu.
mutal karu uri eṉa muṉṉē muṟippaṭuttik kāṭṭiyavar mutaṟporuḷ vakiyiyalpukaḷi mutaliṟ kūṟi, atiyaṭuttuk karupporuḷ kūṟāmal uripporuḷkaḷiṉ iyal kūṟiṉār, iviyiraṇṭum aḷavu paṭṭamiyum iyipu nōkki. avviyipi valiyuṟuttum poruṭṭē īṇṭu iccūttirattāl muṭintatu kāṭṭit tantuṇipurittār. uripporuḷaṉittum tali makkaḷiṉ aṉpiṉintiṇi vakikaḷil amintaṭaṅkum. mutaṟporuḷkaḷum nilampoḻuteṉum iraṇṭē vakiyilaṭaṅkum. iviyiraṇṭum pōlātu karupporuḷvakikaḷ eṇṇiṟantu ēṟṟapeṟṟi viriyumiyalpuṭiyavātalāl, aḷavupaṭṭa iviyiraṇṭum muṟkūṟi, ivaṟṟiṉpiṉ iviyallāta aḷaviṟantaṉavāy varum palavum karupporuḷ vakikaḷāku meṉpatu viḷaṅkak karupporuḷiyal iṟutiyiṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. iv aḷavu muṟiyāṉ vippu muṟiyum amikiṉṟatu. mutaṟporuḷ iraṇṭē vakikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkuvatākaliṉ mutalil vikkap paṭṭatu. uripporuḷkaḷ akavoḻukkaṅkaḷuḷ cilavāka aintu vakikaḷil aṭaṅkutalāl avaṟṟi mutaṟporuḷiṉ piṉṉark kūṟi, avviru vaki yaḷavāṉum aṭaṅkāmal palapaṭaviriyum karupporuḷkaḷi avviraṇṭiṉ piṉ amittuk kūṟiṉār.
cūttiram:18
teyva muṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
avvaki piṟavuṅ karuveṉa moḻipa.
karuttu: itu mutal uripporuḷkaḷil aṭaṅkāvāy akattiṇikku uṟuppām karupporuḷvaki uṇarttukiṟatu.
poruḷ: teyvam = vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuṭpakuti; uṇāvē = ūṇvaki; mā = vilaṅkuvaki; maram = marañceṭikoṭi vaki; puḷ = paṟavi vaki; paṟi = avvannilaṅkaḷukkuriya paṟivakikaḷ; ceyti = toḻilvaki; yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii = yāḻ vakikaḷōṭu kūṭṭi; avvaki piṟavum = avipōla akat
tiṇikaḷukkuc cārpāka vakippaṭuvaṉa maṟṟiyaṉavum; karueṉa
moḻipa = karupporuḷkaḷkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar akapporuṇulār,
kuṟippu: muṉṉic cūttirak kuṟippuriyil cuṭṭiyāṅku, mutalum uriyumallāta akapporuḷkaḷuḷ akattiṇikkuk karuvāy amivaṉa yāvum karupporuḷkaḷ eṉpar akap poruḷ nūlār. iccūttirattil aḷavaṟṟa karupporuḷvaki kaḷaṉittiyum vakuttu niṟuttal kūṭāmiyiṉ, ciṟantaṉa cila cuṭṭi avvaki piṟavum karu eṉṟu kūṟi moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉiyum muṭṭiṉṟu muṭiyavittār tolkāppiyar.
cūttiram: 19
ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum.
karuttu: itu karupporuṇ mayakkamum kaṭitalilli yeṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
poruḷ: ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum = enta nilac cārpil kūṟappaṭum pū, puḷ mutaliya karupporuḷkaḷum; annilam poḻutoṭu vārāvāyiṉum = avvak karupporuṭkuc ciṟanturiya nilattōṭum poḻutōṭum iyintu vantilavāyiṉum; vanta nilattiṉ payatta ākum = kūṟappaṭṭa oḻukkanikaḻiṭat toṭu porunta ataṟkēṟṟa payaṉ taruvaṉavāy amiyum.
kuṟippu: pūvum puḷḷum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ ummi eṇṇummi. vārāvāyiṉum eṉpatilummi yetirmaṟi cuṭṭum; karup poruḷkaḷ perumpālum tattamakkuriya nilam poḻutoṭu iyintu varutalē ciṟanta muṟi, orōviṭattu māṟivariṉum vanta iṭattoṭu poruntap payaṉ taruvatāl kaṭiyappaṭāmal amittuk koḷḷappaṭum eṉum poruṭṭātaliṉ.
uripporuḷkaḷip pōlavē karupporuḷkaḷum mutaṟ poruḷkaḷāṉa nilampoḻutukaḷiṉ vakikaḷil iyipunōkki ēṟṟapeṟṟi orōvoṉṟiṟkē ciṟappurimiyuṭaṉ poruntu vaṉavākum. nilampoḻutu vakikaḷiṉ poruttam nōkki avvavaṟṟiṟkuriya karupporuḷkaḷamiyak kūṟuvatē peruvaḻakkām. eṉiṉum, orōviṭattu nilampoḻutukaḷukku nērurimiyallāta tiṇikkuriya voḻukkaṅkaḷ mayaṅkakkūṟuvatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻak keṉṟu mēlē 12, 13 āvatu cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟiṉatāl, avviṭaṅ kaḷil tammuḷiyipiṉṟi māṟupaṭṭa mutalurip poruḷkaḷil etaṟkiyiyak karupporuḷkaḷiṉ amivu karutappaṭumeṉṟa  aiyam eḻuvatiyalpu. avviyamakaṟṟa īṇṭu iccūttiram eḻuntatu. nilam poḻutukaḷōṭu poruntak karupporuḷ kūṟuvatu peruvaḻakkiṟṟāyiṉum, payaṉnōkki akattiṇiyiṟ ciṟanta oḻukkaṅkaḷukkiyiya nalantaruvaṉavaṟṟi amittuk kōṭal pāṭalcāṉṟa pulaṉeṟivaḻakkēyām eṉpati iccūttiram valiyuṟuttukiṟatu. pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavi nāṭuṅkāli mutaliṟ karuvum karuvil uriyumē muṟi ciṟantaṉa eṉun  tamiḻmarapi muṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttirattuk kūṟiṉarātaliṉ, nilampoḻutukaḷilum ciṟanta uripporuḷoḻukkattiṟkup poruntavarum karup poruḷkaḷ mutaṟporuḷukkiyiyāviṭattum kaṭiyappaṭā eṉṟu  itaṉāl innūlār amivukāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
māyōṉmēya eṉum muṉṉic cūttirattil nāṉilaṅkaḷ muṟiyē mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal eṉat tiṇippeyarē koḷḷum eṉṟamittatum akattuṟikaḷil tiṇikkuriya poruḷkaḷāṉa oḻukkaṅkaḷē ciṟappuṭiyavātal paṟṟiyēyām. āṉāl oru nilattiṟ piṟitoḻukkam nikaḻvatāyp pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañ ceyyumiṭattu avvannilattoṭu iyaṟ porutta millāta karupporuḷkaḷit tiṇinōkkik kūṟanēriṉ, nikaḻumoḻukkattukku akkarupporuḷkaḷ ēṟpuṭiyaṉavāy amiyum payaṉuṭittātal vēṇṭumeṉṟu innūlār īṇṭu oppakkūṟiyamittār. kāṭu, mali, ūr, kaṭal eṉpavaṟṟuḷ kūṟuvatu yātāyiṉum ataṟkiyiyap poruttamuṭiya karupporuḷ kaḷamiyak kūṟutaloṉṟu, aṉṟi annilattil nikaḻvatāyk kūṟappaṭum tiṇikkup porunta amivatoṉṟām. karupporuḷkaḷ tamakkuriya nilam poḻutukaḷōṭu iyiya vārā viṭaṅkaḷil, avvannilattu nikaḻum oḻukkattōṭu amiyum payaṉuṭiyavātal vēṇṭum. īṇṭu nilattiṉ payatta eṉpatu nikaḻum oḻukka nilattik kuṟikkum. aḵtāvatu, annilattil nikaḻvatāyk kūṟappaṭum tiṇiyiccuṭṭum. (neytaṟ) kaṭaṟ karupporuḷkaḷik kāṭṭoṭu cērakkūṟumiṭaṅkaḷil, ap poruḷkaḷ kāṭṭiṟkuriya payaṉtarumeṉpatu iccūttirak karuttaṉṟu; kāṭṭil neytaṟṟiṇi nikaḻumiṭattuk kāṭṭiṟkuriya vallāk karupporuḷkaḷvariṉ, avi āṅkē neytaṟṟiṇik kēṟpaṉavāy amiyak kūṟuvatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkām eṉpatē  iccūttiram nutaliya poruḷākum.
	poriyarik kōṅkiṉ poṉmaruḷ pacuvī
	viriyiṇar vēṅkiyoṭu vēṟupaṭa milicci
	viravumala raṇinta vēṉiṟ kāṉyāṟṟut
	tēroṭu kuṟuka vantōṉ
	pēroṭu puṇarntaṉ ṟaṉṉiyiva ḷuyirē	(aiṅkuṟu. 367)
itil, pālikkuriya vēṉiṟkālattu, mullik kāṉyāṟṟil, puṇarvuttiṇiyil, pālikkōṅkiṉ pacumalarōṭu kuṟiñci vēṅkippūvum viravip puṇarntu pirintōr oḻukkoṭu poruntavanta cevviyum  vāyppum tikaḻvatu kāṇka.
cūttiram: 20
peyarum viṉiyumeṉ ṟāyiru vakiya
tiṇitoṟu marīiya tiṇinilip peyarē.
karuttu: itu, karupporuḷkaḷōṭu aṭivuṭiya akat tiṇik kuriyarāṉa avvan nilamakkaḷiṉ peyarppākupāṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: tiṇitoṟu marīiya tiṇinilip peyarē = ovvō roḻukkattoṭum poruntiya mulli mutaliya nilaṅ kaḷil pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil akattiṇikkuriyarāyk kūṟappaṭum makkaṭpeyarkaḷ; peyarum viṉiyumeṉṟāyiruvakiya = avvannilattāṉamiyum peyarppeyarum, annilattu makkaḷiṉ toḻilāṉ amiyum viṉippeyarum, eṉṟu avviraṇṭu kūṟupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyavākum.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram acinili. peyarum viṉiyum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ ummi eṇṇummi. tiṇinili eṉpatu ākupeyarāyt tiṇiniliyiṉarik kuṟikkum.
nāṉilaṅkaḷiṉum uḷḷa makkaḷ, akattiṇikkuriyarāyc ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭuṅkāl, mulli mutaliya avvattiṇi nilaṅkaḷukkuriya iyal  iyalpuṭiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu; avvāṟaṉṟit tattam toḻiṟkiyipuṭiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu; avvāṟaṉṟit tattam toḻiṟkiyipuṭiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu. ivvirumuṟikaḷē tamiḻakat tolli marapoṭu aṭivuṭiyavākum. tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻulakil tamiḻariṭip piṟappaḷavil eṉṟum uyarvu tāḻvuṭaṉ vēṟupāṭuṭiya cātivakuppukkaḷ kiṭiyā. ataṉāl akkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ tattam nilattukkēṟṟāṅku āyar, kuṟavar, uḻavar, maṟavar eṉṟaḻikkap
paṭṭārkaḷ.
iṉi, oru nilattuḷḷār annilattiṟkuriya toḻil mēṟ koḷḷātu piṟitu toḻil kiyāḷuvarāyiṉ, avaravar cey toḻil vēṟṟumiyāl toḻil kuṟikkum ēṟpuṭi viṉip peyarkaḷālum aḻikkappaṭuvar. nuḷiñar, paratavar eṉpaṉa neytaṉilañcuṭṭiya makkaṭpeyar. valiyar, umaṇar eṉpaṉa nilañcuṭṭātu, valivīcip piḻikkun toḻiluṭiyāriyum, uppu uṇṭākki viṟkum toḻiluṭiyāriyum cuṭṭum viṉip peyarkaḷ. toḻil etupurintum neytaṉilam vatipavarip paratavar allatu nuḷiñar eṉavaḻaṅkal oru muṟi; appōtu appeyar neytaṉilamakkaḷ eṉṉum poruṭṭākum. iṉi ennilattuṟiyiṉum valit toḻil purintu vāḻvār eṉpatik kuṟikkuṅkāl, valiyar eṉṉum viṉippeyarāl attoḻiluṭiyāric cuṭṭuvatu orumuṟi. tamiḻakattiṟ paṇṭik kālattil piṟappāl cāti vakuppukkaḷ illāmiyāṉum, tamiḻ makkaḷellārum virumpiyāṅkut tattamakkēṟpuṭiya toḻil koḷḷum urimi yuṭiyarātalālum, nilam toḻil vakikaḷāl vēṟupaṭiṉum uṇavu maṇaṅkaḷil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yāṇṭum ellārum ēṟṟa peṟṟi kalantu oṉṟi vāḻntārātalāṉum, avar akavoḻukkaṅ kūṟum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil akkālattavariṭi nilipēṟuṟṟa nilappeyar viṉippeyarkaḷāl tamiḻ makkaḷ aḻikkappaṭum marapuṇmiyit tolkāppiyar īṇṭu viḷakkip pōntār. nilampaṟṟiya makkaṭ peyar, peyarp peyar, ceyyum viṉi paṟṟiya makkaṭpeyar viṉippeyar.
akam 110ām pāṭṭil, pōntip pacaliyār, tuḷicci yit taliviyākavum, neṭuntērūraṉit talivaṉākavum tiṇinilippeyar amittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār naṟṟiṇi 45ām pāṭṭil,
	neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku niyama mūtūrk
	kaṭuntērc celvaṉ kātaṟ ṟalimakaṉākavum,
niṇaccuṟā vaṟutta vuṇakkal vēṇṭi, yiṉappuḷ ḷōppum pulavunāṟum paravarmakaḷ talimakaḷākavum, ivvāṟu ivviru vēṟu nilamakkaḷ kātaṟṟalimakkaḷāy akattiṇikkuriyarāyamin tiruppatu iṅkuk karutattakkatu. akam 280ām pāṭṭil, paratavar makaḷit taliviyākavum, piṟitoru nilamakaṉit talivaṉākavum, amittu ammūvaṉār kūṟiyatum akkālat tamiḻmarapu atuvāta lāṉ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. iṉṉa pala paḻampāṭṭukkaḷāl paṇṭit tamiḻakattil nāṉila makkaḷuḷ maṇamum uṇavum piṟappu nili viṉikaḷ paṟṟi variyappaṭāmal kalantu kiyāḷap
paṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teḷḷatteḷiyak koḷḷappaṭum. marapiyalil kāṇap peṟum varuṇavakipaṟṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ iṭic cerukal eṉpatu piṟāṇṭu viḷakkappaṭum.
	yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō?
	entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷir?
	yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi yaṟitum?
	cempulap peyalnīr pōla
	aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokip (40ām) pāṭṭāṉum piṟappu toḻil vaki karutāmal otta aṉpē maṇattiṟkup pōtiyatu eṉṉun tamiḻmarapu viḷakkamākum.
nāṉilat tamiḻmakkaḷum tammuḷ vēṟupāṭiṉṟi maṇantu koḷḷum paḻiya vaḻakkuṇmi, cāti veṟiyalikkum piṟkālak kōvikaḷum palanila makkaḷiṉ kalappumaṇam kuṟittut tamiḻmarapu vaḻuvāmal akattuṟikaḷamittuk kūṟuvatālum valuppeṟukiṉṟatu.
	kaḻikōṭu villiyic ceṟṟār tiyākar kamaliveṟpiṉ
	uḻikōṭi cuṟṟuṅ kiriyema tūruma tūrmarutam;
	taḻikōṭi koṇṭu camittatem māṭi taṉittaṉiyō?
	iḻikōṭi poṉpeṟu mēyuma tāṭi yiṟiyavarē
			(ellappanāyiṉār tiruvārūrk kōvi. cey. 101)
ivvuṇmiyi maṟantu tolkāppiyar nūliṉ urikārar ippaṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇa (nūṟ) cūttirattiṟku, avarkāla iyipaṟṟa purāṇak katikaḷiyum iyalvaḻakkaṟṟa āriyak koḷkikaḷiyum pukuttip, poruntāp putturikaḷ kūṟi mayaṅkavittār. āriyaruḷ nāṉku varuṇattārukkumē tamiḻariṉ akattiṇik kaḷaviyal  oḻukka āṭciyurimi avar tam taruma cāttiraṅkaḷilum vaḻakkilum iṉmiyāṉum, āriya taruma nūlkaḷkūṟum uyarpiṟap purimiyuṭiya iṭiyiruvaṇattār tamiḻakatteṉṟu millāmiyāṉum, ivarurikaḷ poruntāmi yaṟika. aṉṟiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇanūl......
 mūvar taṇpoḻil varippil nāṟpeyarelli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu eṉat tolkāppiyar tāmum, vaṭa vēṅkaṭan teṉkumariyāyiṭit tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattuvaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭic,centamiḻiyaṟkacivaṇiyanilattoṭumuntunūlkaṇṭumuṟippaṭeṇṇip pulan tokuttōṉa... tolkāppiyaṉ, eṉat tolkāppiyariṉ pāyiramum vaṟpuṟuttuvatāṉum, iṅkut tolkāppiyariṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippaṉa ellām āriyavaruṇa aṟaṅkaḷiyalla, tamiḻ marapum tamiḻar vaḻakkaṅkaḷumē yāmeṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivvuṇmikaḷ iṉivaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷukkum okkum.cūttiram: 21
āyar vēṭṭuvar āṭūut tiṇippeyar;
āvayiṉ varūuṅ kiḻavaru muḷarē.
karuttu: itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟ kūṟiya tiṇi nilippeyar vakikaḷum appeyaruṭiyāriṉ akattiṇikkurimiyuṅ viḷakkukiṟatu.
poruḷ: āyar vēṭṭuvar āṭūuttiṇippeyar= āyarn vaṭṭuvar eṉpaṉa āṇpāl cuṭṭum mulli nilamakkaḷiṉ tiṇi nilappeyarkaḷām; āvayiṉ varūumkiḻavarumuḷarē= annilattuakattiṇikkurimibkāḷpavarumuḷarām.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram acinili.
āyar eṉpatu mullinila makkaḷukkut tiṇippeyarākum. atuvēpōl, vēṭṭuvar eṉpatum mullinila makkaṭ peyareṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum, kuṟiñci nila makkaṭ peyareṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar. ivaruḷ iḷampūraṇar kūṟṟup poruṭ ciṟappuṭiyatu. māyōṉ mēya eṉṉum muṉ 5āñ cūttirattil tiṇinilaṅkaḷil mutalil vitteṇṇiya mulli nila makkaḷukkuriya peyarkaḷuḷ, āyareṉṉum nilappeyarum vēṭṭuvareṉṉum viṉippeyarum annilat tāṇmakkaḷiṉ tiṇippeyarkaḷāka iccūttirattil tolkāppiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāreṉak koḷvatē peyarum viṉiyu meṉṉum mēṟcūttirak karutti viḷakkuvataṟku aṭivupaṭa amivatākum. vēṭṭuvareṉpatu mullit tiṇimakkaḷiṉ vēṭṭu viṉi cuṭṭun tiṇippeyarākum. ataṉāl, innūlār mēlē tiṇi nilippeyar peyarum viṉiyumeṉṟāyiruvakiya eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyati, iccūttirattil vakuttu meynniṟuttu viḷakkiṉār.
itil tiṇippeyareṉṟum, itaṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varum cūttiraṅkaḷil tiṇinilip peyareṉṟum, varuvaṉa akavoḻukkattuk kurimikoḷvār peyariyē kuṟikkum. akkālat tamiḻ marapukkum, uṇmiyulakiyal vaḻakkukku mēṟpat, tamiḻ makkaḷellārum akattiṇit tuṟikaḷil kātaṟṟali makkaḷātaṟ kuriyar eṉpati innūlār iṅkup palacūttiraṅkaḷāṟ teḷikkiṉṟār. nāṭāṭcikkuriyarē akattiṇik kiḷavit talimakkaḷ āvataṟkuriyar pōlavum, allāta nāṉila
makkaḷum viṉivalar aṭiyār mutalāyiṉārum aṉpiṉintiṇit tuṟikaḷil kiḷavittalivar. ākār pōlavum, poruḷpaṭumāṟu iccūttiraṅkaḷukkup piṟar kūṟumuri poruntātu. talimakkaḷ eṉpatu īṇṭu akattiṇik kiḷavit talimakkaḷiyē kuṟikkum; nāṭāṭcit talimi kuṟippatu īṇṭikku vēṇṭappaṭā.
paratti orutti, niyamamūtūrk kaṭuntērccelvaṉ makaṉiṉ meykkātali yikaḻntu, yām pulavu nāṟutum celaniṉṟīmō; (kaṭaliṉ) perunīr viḷiyuḷeñ ciṟunal vāḻkki nummoṭu purivatō? aṉṟē eṉa maṟuppavaḷ, tamakkēṟṟa talivartam miṉattavaruḷḷumuḷar eṉat teḷittu, emmaṉōriṟ cemmalu muṭittē (naṟ. 45) eṉa viḷakkum tarukkuri, tamiḻar evarēṉum akattuṟiyiṟ ṟalimakka ḷurimiyaṉittu muṭiyar eṉṉu muṇmiyi valiyuṟuttutal kāṇka. iṉṉum itupaṟṟi mēṟ cūttira uriyiṟ kāṭṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ, piṉvarum paṇṭic cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷaṭikaḷālum tamiḻariṭi nilam toḻil nili piṟappu vakikaḷāl akattiṇikkurimi yārukkum vilakkilli yeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. nellum uppum nērēyūrīr, koḷḷīrōveṉac cēritōṟum uppuc cumantu viṟṟuttiriyum umaṇmakaḷit taliviyākki, ammūvaṉār kūṟiya akam 390 ām pāṭṭu ivvuṇmiyi valiyuṟuttum.
.rhu‰ ciṟukuṭik kuṟavaṉ peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷit taliviyākkum mālvari eṉṉum kapilar kuṟumpāṭṭum, (kuṟun. 95)
variyakac ciṟutiṉic cevvāyp pāciṉam kaṭiyum koṭicciyit talimakaḷākkum naṟṟiṇi (134ām) pāṭṭum,
iṉṉum ituvēpōl pala paḻampāṭṭukkaḷum piṟappāṟ ciṟappetuvumillāta āyar, kuṟavar nuḷiyar mutaliya yārum kātaṟṟalimakka ḷātaṟkuriyar eṉpatit teḷivākkum.

		pūntā marippōtu tanta viravuttārk
